## `AFG-0220-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Scaling Inspector
- **Scenario:** Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Log Scaling, Defect Identification & Timber Grader
- **Intro:** Scaling Inspector — Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Log Scaling, Defect Identification & Timber Grader — Receive Batch Documentation and Verify Lot Number: Lot number must match exactly; quantity tolerance ±2% of declared volume; species must match declared species code.
